// TODO(GEX-1292): Support parameterization interfaces for this op. Not needed
// yet: it is only created by a pass that copies its shape from an existing
// mo.buffer.
def MO_BufferCreateOp : MO_Op<"buffer.create", [
  MO_PreservedDuringKernelLowering,
  Pure,
]> {
  let summary = "Creates a buffer on a specific device, optionally initialized.";
  let description = [{
    Creates a buffer of the result type. By default it is uninitialized and
    reallocated on every execution.

    With `initValue` (same element type as the buffer), the buffer is
    persistent: allocated and filled once during the model's `init` phase,
    then reused across `execute` calls. This gives kernels scratch state that
    survives calls, such as a counter the kernel re-zeroes after each run.

    Example:
    ```mlir
    %buf = mo.buffer.create : !mo.buffer<[20, 20], f32, gpu:0>
    %zeroed = mo.buffer.create [initValue = 0.0 : f32] : !mo.buffer<[20, 20], f32, gpu:0>
    ```
  }];

  let arguments = (ins
    // Single-element elements attr of the buffer's dtype (verified).
    OptionalAttr<AnyAttr>:$initValue
  );
  let results = (outs MO_Buffer:$result);

  let assemblyFormat = [{
    (` ` `[` `initValue` `=` $initValue^ `]`)? attr-dict `:`
    qualified(type($result))
  }];
  let hasVerifier = 1;

  // The `{}` default lets Python bindings omit `initValue`; MLIR attribute
  // casters reject `None`.
  let skipDefaultBuilders = 1;
  let builders = [
    OpBuilder<(ins "Type":$result,
                   CArg<"::mlir::Attribute", "{}">:$initValue)>,
  ];
}

def MO_MutableStoreSliceOp : MO_MutableOp<"mutable.store.slice", [
  MO_SingleDeviceWithHostOperands<
    /*hostOperands*/["start", "stop", "step"],
    /*deviceOperands*/["inBuffer", "slice"]>,
]> {
  let summary = "Stores a tensor to a slice of a buffer semantic tensor.";
  let description = [{
    Stores `slice` into the elements of `inBuffer` selected by the per-dimension
    `start`, `stop`, and `step` vectors, with the same indexing semantics as
    `mo.slice`.

    Example:
    ```mlir
      %buffer: !mo.buffer<[20, 20], f32>
      %slice: !mo.tensor<[D0, D1], f32>
      %ch: !mo.chain

      %start: !mo.tensor<[2], si64> // [1,  -6]
      %stop: !mo.tensor<[2], si32>  // [-3, -3]
      %step: !mo.tensor<[2], si64>  // [5,   1]

      // equivalent to this in numpy: `buffer[1:-3:5, -6:-3:1] = slice`
      %ch' = mo.mutable.store.slice(%ch, %buffer, %slice, %start, %stop, %step)
    ```

    The output chain has at most one use.
  }];
  let arguments = (ins
    MO_Buffer:$inBuffer,
    MO_Tensor:$slice,
    MO_IndicesVectorIfRanked:$start,
    MO_IndicesVectorIfRanked:$stop,
    MO_IndicesVectorIfRanked:$step,
    MO_Chain:$inChain,
    MO_ParamDecls:$outputParamDecls
  );
  let results = (outs MO_Chain:$outChain);
  let assemblyFormat = [{
    `` custom<ParamDecls>($outputParamDecls) `(` operands `)` attr-dict `:`
    functional-type(operands, results)
  }];
  let hasVerifier = 1;
}

//===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//
// Collective ops
//===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//

class MO_DistributedOp<string name, list<Trait> traits = []> :
  MO_TensorOp<name, [
    DeclareOpInterfaceMethods<MO_MutableOpInterface>,
    DeclareOpInterfaceMethods<MemoryEffectsOpInterface>,
    MO_SameVariadicOperandSize,
    MO_SameVariadicResultSize,
  ] # traits> {
  let arguments = (ins Variadic<MO_Tensor>:$inputs);
  let results = (outs Variadic<MO_Tensor>:$outputs);
  let assemblyFormat = [{
    `(` operands `)` attr-dict `:` functional-type(operands, results)
  }];
}

def MO_AllReduceSumOp : MO_DistributedOp<"distributed.allreduce.sum", [
  AllTypesMatch<["inputs", "outputs"]>,
  MO_MultiDevice,
]> {
  let summary = "Allreduce sum across N devices.";
  let description = [{
    Sums same-shaped inputs, one per device, across devices.

    A nonzero `group_size` smaller than the input count splits devices into
    contiguous independent groups (e.g. TP groups under DP); shapes then only
    need to match within a group.
  }];
  let hasVerifier = 1;
  let hasFolder = 1;
  let arguments = (ins
    Variadic<MO_Tensor>:$inputs,
    Variadic<MO_Buffer>:$signalBuffers,
    MO_Chain:$inChain,
    DefaultValuedAttr<I64Attr, "0">:$group_size
  );
  let results = (outs
    Variadic<MO_Tensor>:$outputs,
    MO_Chain:$outChain
  );
  let assemblyFormat = [{
    `(` operands `)` (` ` `[` `group_size` `=` $group_size^ `]`)? attr-dict `:`
    functional-type(operands, results)
  }];
}

def MO_BundledExpandOp : Op<MODialect, "bundled.expand", [
  MO_BundledCollective,
  MO_PreservedDuringKernelLowering,
  Pure,
]> {
  let summary = "Expand a per-device tensor to all launches' tensors.";
  let description = [{
    Inside an `mo.parallel` body, expands a single-device tensor (typically a
    block argument) into one tensor per launch, making collective N-expansion
    explicit in the IR. Results match the input's shape and dtype, one per
    launch, on each launch's device (from the first input bundle).

    Example:
    ```mlir
    mo.parallel (%arg) in (%dt : !mo.bundle<[!mo.tensor<[3], f32, gpu:0>,
                                              !mo.tensor<[3], f32, gpu:1>]>)
        -> (!mo.bundle<[...]>) {
      %peer0, %peer1 = mo.bundled.expand(%arg)
          : !mo.tensor<[3], f32, gpu:0>
         -> (!mo.tensor<[3], f32, gpu:0>, !mo.tensor<[3], f32, gpu:1>)
      // ... use %peer0, %peer1 as inputs to a collective ...
    }
    ```
  }];
  let arguments = (ins MO_Tensor:$input);
  let results = (outs Variadic<MO_Tensor>:$results);
  let assemblyFormat = [{
    `(` $input `)` attr-dict `:` type($input) `->` `(` type($results) `)`
  }];
  let hasVerifier = 1;
  let hasFolder = 1;
}

def MO_BundledAllReduceSumOp : MO_TensorOp<"bundled.allreduce.sum", [
  DeclareOpInterfaceMethods<MO_MutableOpInterface>,
  DeclareOpInterfaceMethods<MemoryEffectsOpInterface>,
  MO_BundledCollective,
  MO_SameVariadicOperandSize,
  MO_SingleDeviceWithPeerInputs<>,
]> {
  let summary = "Per-device allreduce sum, for use inside mo.parallel.";
  let description = [{
    Per-device allreduce sum inside `mo.parallel`. Takes N peer tensors (from
    `mo.bundled.expand`), N signal buffers captured from graph scope (chain
    guarded by the parent's `buffers(...)` clause), and a chain.

    Example:
    ```mlir
    mo.parallel (%arg) in (%dt : !mo.bundle<[...]>)
        buffers(%sig0 : ..., %sig1 : ...) chain(%ch) -> (...) {
      %peer0, %peer1 = mo.bundled.expand(%arg)
          : !mo.tensor<[3], f32, gpu:0>
         -> (!mo.tensor<[3], f32, gpu:0>, !mo.tensor<[3], f32, gpu:1>)
      %out, %ch_out = mo.bundled.allreduce.sum(
          %peer0, %peer1, %sig0, %sig1, %ch)
          : (!mo.tensor<[3], f32, gpu:0>, !mo.tensor<[3], f32, gpu:1>,
             !mo.buffer<[1], ui8, gpu:0>, !mo.buffer<[1], ui8, gpu:1>,
             !mo.chain)
          -> (!mo.tensor<[3], f32, gpu:0>, !mo.chain)
      mo.yield %out : !mo.tensor<[3], f32, gpu:0>
    }
    ```
  }];
  let hasFolder = 1;
  let arguments = (ins
    Variadic<MO_Tensor>:$inputs,
    Variadic<MO_Buffer>:$signalBuffers,
    MO_Chain:$inChain
  );
  let results = (outs
    MO_Tensor:$output,
    MO_Chain:$outChain
  );
  let assemblyFormat = [{
    `(` operands `)` attr-dict `:` functional-type(operands, results)
  }];
}

//===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//
// BundledBroadcastOp

def MO_BundledBroadcastOp : MO_TensorOp<"bundled.broadcast", [
  DeclareOpInterfaceMethods<MO_MutableOpInterface>,
  DeclareOpInterfaceMethods<MemoryEffectsOpInterface>,
  MO_BundledCollective,
  MO_SingleDeviceWithPeerInputs<>,
]> {
  let summary = "Per-device broadcast of the root device's tensor.";
  let description = [{
    The per-device form of `mo.distributed.broadcast`, one op per
    participating device: copies `input`, the root device's tensor, into
    this device's output. Every copy, the root's included, reads `input`,
    which the device-graph lowering routes through a stable address slot.
    `root` is the root's index in the signal buffer list, which holds one
    buffer per device.
  }];
  let hasVerifier = 1;
  let hasFolder = 1;
  let arguments = (ins
    MO_Tensor:$input,
    Variadic<MO_Buffer>:$signalBuffers,
    MO_Chain:$inChain,
    DefaultValuedAttr<I64Attr, "0">:$root
  );
  let results = (outs
    MO_Tensor:$output,
    MO_Chain:$outChain
  );
  let assemblyFormat = [{
    `(` operands `)` (` ` `[` `root` `=` $root^ `]`)? attr-dict `:`
    functional-type(operands, results)
  }];
}

def MO_BundledAllGatherOp : MO_TensorOp<"bundled.allgather", [
  DeclareOpInterfaceMethods<MO_MutableOpInterface>,
  DeclareOpInterfaceMethods<MemoryEffectsOpInterface>,
  MO_BundledCollective,
  MO_SameVariadicOperandSize,
  MO_SameVariadicResultSize,
  MO_SingleDeviceWithPeerInputs<>,
]> {
  let summary = "Per-device allgather over every peer's tensor.";
  let description = [{
    The per-device form of `mo.distributed.allgather`, one op per device and
    full world only: takes every peer's tensor, which the device-graph
    lowering routes through stable address slots, and one signal buffer per
    device, and produces this device's gathered pieces, one per peer in peer
    order, each with the peer's shape on this device. A grouped allgather
    routes part of a shard through a paired group's buffers, which a device
    graph cannot express, so `group_size` has no per-device form.
  }];
  let hasVerifier = 1;
  let hasFolder = 1;
  let arguments = (ins
    Variadic<MO_Tensor>:$inputs,
    Variadic<MO_Buffer>:$signalBuffers,
    MO_Chain:$inChain
  );
  let results = (outs
    Variadic<MO_Tensor>:$outputs,
    MO_Chain:$outChain
  );
  let assemblyFormat = [{
    `(` operands `)` attr-dict `:` functional-type(operands, results)
  }];
}

// device, so execution devices come from the outputs.

  DeclareOpInterfaceMethods<MemoryEffectsOpInterface>,
  MO_HasDeviceInterface,
  MO_MultiDeviceFrom<"outputs">,
  MO_TensorOpInterface,
]> {

    Replicates `input` from the root device to all participating devices.
    `root` is the root's index in the signal buffer list.

  let hasFolder = 1;
  let arguments = (ins
    MO_Tensor:$input,
    Variadic<MO_Buffer>:$signalBuffers,

  );
  let results = (outs
    Variadic<MO_Tensor>:$outputs,
    MO_Chain:$outChain
  );
  let assemblyFormat = [{
    `(` operands `)` (` ` `[` `root` `=` $root^ `]`)? attr-dict `:`
    functional-type(operands, results)
  }];
}

// TODO: Make into custom ops once custom ops can be marked CPU- or GPU-only;
// device specificity is currently tied to individual ops.

/// Info needed by `max.experimental.torch` to register a Mojo kernel as a
/// `torch.library.custom_op`. Built from the kernel's `execute` signature
/// via `M::MOGG::KernelDeclAdaptor`, with the torch-side type policy applied
/// (regular tensors and `std::SIMD` accepted as `torch.Tensor` parameters,
/// device contexts ignored, everything else rejected).
struct KernelTorchInfo {
  size_t numDpsOutputs;
  /// Source names of tensor-like arguments in declaration order
  /// (DPS outputs first, then inputs). Used to build the synthesised
  /// `inspect.Signature` for the registered torch op.
  std::vector<std::string> tensorArgNames;
};

/// Get the currently active MLIR context from Python
mlir::MLIRContext *current_context();

void bindBuiltinDialect(nanobind::module_);
void bindKGENDialect(nanobind::module_);

void bindMODialect(nanobind::module_);

void bindRMODialect(nanobind::module_);

void bindDriverModule(nanobind::module_);
void bindDTypeModule(nanobind::module_);
void bindEngineModule(nanobind::module_);
void bindGraphModule(nanobind::module_);
void bindProfilerModule(nanobind::module_);
void bindNIXLModule(nanobind::module_);
void bindSafetensorsModule(nanobind::module_);
void bindXXHashModule(nanobind::module_);
void bindXGrammarModule(nanobind::module_);
void bindMetricsModule(nanobind::module_);
void bindRequestContextModule(nanobind::module_);
void bindDLPackModule(nanobind::module_);

//===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//
// KernelLibrary: Python-facing wrapper over a standalone `ModuleOp` populated
// with `mogg.kernel.decl` / `mogg.primitive.decl` ops by the
// `--mogg-import-packages` pass. Replaces the older `MojoLibraryAnalysis`
// side-table.
//===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//

class Analysis : std::enable_shared_from_this<Analysis> {
public:
  Analysis(nanobind::object context, std::vector<std::filesystem::path> paths);
  std::vector<std::string> symbolNames();
  std::vector<std::filesystem::path> libraryPaths();
  mlir::Operation *kernelOp(const std::string &name);
  void verifyCustomOp(MlirOperation op);
  void addPath(std::filesystem::path path);
  // Copy `mogg.kernel.decl` / `mogg.primitive.decl` ops and the
  // `mogg.opaque_type_mapping` attribute from the analysis's standalone
  // module into `target`. Called by the compile path on a model's MLIR
  // module (a `ModuleOp` Operation) just before invoking the graph
  // compiler.
  void seedKernelDecls(MlirOperation target);
  // Walk the named kernel's `execute` signature via `KernelDeclAdaptor` and
  // return the torch-side info: number of DPS outputs and the source names
  // of tensor-like arguments. Throws `nb::value_error` if `name` is not a
  // registered kernel or if any argument has a type the torch path can't
  // marshal as a `torch.Tensor`.
  KernelTorchInfo kernelTorchInfo(const std::string &name);
  /// Whether the op bound to `name` has a registered `mogg.shape_func.decl`.
  bool hasShapeFunction(const std::string &name);

private:
  // Keep the Python MLIR context alive at least as long as the standalone
  // module; the module's ops reference types/attributes owned by the context.
  nanobind::object context;
  // Cumulative set of registered package paths. Maintained client-side so
  // `libraryPaths()` doesn't have to re-derive it from the module.
  std::set<std::filesystem::path> libraryPathsSet;
  // Lazily built on first access; holds the `mogg.kernel.decl` and
  // `mogg.primitive.decl` ops produced by `--mogg-import-packages` for the
  // accumulated paths.
  mlir::OwningOpRef<mlir::ModuleOp> kernelDecls;
  // Lazily built from `kernelDecls` and reused across query / verify calls.
  // Invalidated whenever `addPath` mutates the module. Heap-allocated and
  // forward-declared so this header doesn't drag the GC `MojoRegistry`
  // header (and its transitive includes) into Python-binding TUs.
  std::unique_ptr<M::MOGG::MojoRegistry> registry;

public:
  // Out-of-line dtor because `MojoRegistry` is only forward-declared above.
  ~Analysis();

private:
  // Ensures `kernelDecls` is populated for all currently-registered paths
  // and returns it.
  mlir::ModuleOp ensureModule();
  // Ensures `registry` is built from the current `kernelDecls` and returns
  // a reference. Each callsite that does multiple registry queries should
  // bind a const-ref once instead of re-calling.
  const M::MOGG::MojoRegistry &ensureRegistry();
};

// `mlir::Pass` has non-inline virtual functions. Since we compile it without
// typeinfo, and the place where C++ defines the typeinfo to be doesn't contain
// it, we need to bind a wrapper class instead.
struct Pass {
  std::unique_ptr<mlir::Pass> pass;
  Pass(std::unique_ptr<mlir::Pass> pass) : pass(std::move(pass)) {}
};

} // namespace M::Graph::Python

#endif // SDK_GRAPHAPI_PYTHON_BINDINGS_H

#include "_core/Graph/Bindings.h"
#include "GraphCompiler/MOGGDialect/Analysis/KernelDeclAdaptor.h"
#include "GraphCompiler/MOGGDialect/Analysis/MojoRegistry.h"
#include "Support/Nanobind/TypeCasters.h" // IWYU pragma: keep (type casters)

#include "mlir/CAPI/IR.h"

namespace nb = nanobind;

mlir::MLIRContext *M::Graph::Python::current_context() {
  static nb::handle context = nb::module_::import_("max.mlir").attr("Context");
  nb::handle current = context.attr("current");
  if (current.is_none())
    throw std::runtime_error("No active MLIR context");
  using Caster = nb::detail::make_caster<mlir::MLIRContext>;
  Caster caster;
  if (!caster.from_python(current, 0, {})) {
    throw std::runtime_error("Failed to infer MLIR context.");
  }
  return caster;
}

std::vector<std::string> M::Graph::Python::Analysis::symbolNames() {
  // Touch ensureRegistry so the cache is hot for later queries, even though
  // we iterate the module directly here (registry doesn't expose iteration).
  (void)ensureRegistry();
  mlir::ModuleOp module = *kernelDecls;
  std::vector<std::string> symbols;
  for (auto decl : module.getOps<M::MOGG::KernelDeclOp>())
    symbols.emplace_back(decl.getRegistration().str());
  for (auto decl : module.getOps<M::MOGG::PrimitiveDeclOp>())
    symbols.emplace_back(decl.getRegistration().str());
  return symbols;
}

std::vector<std::filesystem::path> M::Graph::Python::Analysis::libraryPaths() {
  return std::vector<std::filesystem::path>(libraryPathsSet.begin(),
                                            libraryPathsSet.end());
}

mlir::Operation *M::Graph::Python::Analysis::kernelOp(const std::string &name) {
  const M::MOGG::MojoRegistry &reg = ensureRegistry();
  auto kernels = reg.lookupKernel(name);
  if (!kernels.empty()) {
    M::MOGG::KernelDeclOp kernel = kernels.front();
    return kernel.getOperation();
  }
  if (reg.containsPrimitive(name)) {
    M::MOGG::PrimitiveDeclOp prim = reg.lookupPrimitive(name);
    return prim.getOperation();
  }
  return nullptr;
}

void M::Graph::Python::Analysis::verifyCustomOp(MlirOperation op) {
  auto err = M::MOGG::verifyCustomOp(ensureRegistry(), unwrap(op));
  if (err.isError())
    throw nb::value_error(err.getError());
}

void M::Graph::Python::Analysis::seedKernelDecls(MlirOperation target) {
  auto targetModule = mlir::cast<mlir::ModuleOp>(unwrap(target));
  MAXG_seedKernelDecls(wrap(targetModule), wrap(ensureModule()));
}

namespace {

/// Reformat a `KernelDeclOpaqueAdaptor` dotted path (e.g. `"std.String"`,
/// `"std.utils.index.IndexList"`) as the legacy `"root::leaf"` presentation
/// that the torch error message expects.
std::string toRootLeafTypeName(llvm::StringRef dotted) {
  llvm::StringRef root = dotted.split('.').first;
  llvm::StringRef leaf = dotted.rsplit('.').second;
  if (leaf.empty() || leaf == root)
    return root.str();
  return (root + "::" + leaf).str();
}

/// Stringify a rejected argument's type for the unsupported-arg error.
std::string describeRejectedArg(const M::MOGG::KernelDeclOperandAdaptor &arg) {
  if (const auto *o =
          std::get_if<M::MOGG::KernelDeclOpaqueAdaptor>(&arg.underlyingType))
    return toRootLeafTypeName(o->typeName.strref());
  if (arg.isVariadicTensorType())
    return M::MOGG::kMojoVariadicTensorsName.str();
  return "<unknown>";
}

} // namespace

M::Graph::Python::KernelTorchInfo
M::Graph::Python::Analysis::kernelTorchInfo(const std::string &name) {
  const M::MOGG::MojoRegistry &reg = ensureRegistry();
  auto kernels = reg.lookupKernel(name);
  if (kernels.empty())
    throw nb::value_error(
        llvm::formatv("No kernel registered as '{0}'.", name).str().c_str());

  M::MOGG::KernelDeclAdaptor adaptor(kernels.front());
  if (!adaptor.executeFunction)
    throw nb::value_error(
        llvm::formatv("Kernel '{0}' has no execute function.", name)
            .str()
            .c_str());

  KernelTorchInfo info;
  const M::MOGG::KernelDeclFunctionAdaptor &fn = *adaptor.executeFunction;
  info.numDpsOutputs = fn.outputArguments.size();

  auto collect = [&](const M::MOGG::KernelDeclOperandAdaptor &arg) {
    // Regular tensors and scalar SIMD args become torch.Tensor parameters.
    if ((arg.isTensorType() && !arg.isVariadicTensorType()) ||
        arg.isScalarType()) {
      info.tensorArgNames.push_back(
          arg.sourceDescriptor->sourceName.strref().str());
      return;
    }
    // Device contexts are supplied implicitly by the graph compiler.
    if (arg.isContextType())
      return;
    throw nb::value_error(
        llvm::formatv("Unsupported argument type '{0}' in custom op '{1}'.",
                      describeRejectedArg(arg), name)
            .str()
            .c_str());
  };

  for (const auto &arg : fn.outputArguments)
    collect(arg);
  for (const auto &arg : fn.inputArguments)
    collect(arg);

  return info;
}

bool M::Graph::Python::Analysis::hasShapeFunction(const std::string &name) {
  const M::MOGG::MojoRegistry &reg = ensureRegistry();
  return reg.containsShapeFunction(name);
}

void M::Graph::Python::Analysis::addPath(std::filesystem::path path) {
  auto abs = std::filesystem::absolute(path);
  bool inserted = libraryPathsSet.insert(abs).second;
  if (!inserted)
    return;
  // If the module has already been built, append the new path; otherwise the
  // upcoming `ensureModule` call will pick it up.
  if (kernelDecls) {
    auto err = MAXG_addKernelPackage(wrap(*kernelDecls), abs);
    if (err.isError())
      throw nb::value_error(err.getError());
    // Invalidate the cached registry: the new package's decls aren't in it.
    registry.reset();
  }
}

M::Graph::Python::Analysis::Analysis(nb::object context,
                                     std::vector<std::filesystem::path> paths)
    : context(context) {
  for (const auto &path : paths)
    libraryPathsSet.insert(std::filesystem::absolute(path));
}

M::Graph::Python::Analysis::~Analysis() = default;

const M::MOGG::MojoRegistry &M::Graph::Python::Analysis::ensureRegistry() {
  mlir::ModuleOp module = ensureModule();
  if (!registry)
    registry = std::make_unique<M::MOGG::MojoRegistry>(module);
  return *registry;
}

mlir::ModuleOp M::Graph::Python::Analysis::ensureModule() {
  if (kernelDecls)
    return *kernelDecls;
  MlirContext ctx = nb::cast<MlirContext>(context);
  auto moduleOr = MAXG_createKernelDeclsModule(ctx);
  if (moduleOr.isError())
    throw nb::value_error(moduleOr.getError());
  kernelDecls = moduleOr.takeValue();
  // Load all currently-registered paths into the fresh module. The first
  // call to MAXG_addKernelPackage on an empty module also loads the built-in
  // `kernels` / `primitives` packages; subsequent calls append.
  for (const auto &path : libraryPathsSet) {
    auto err = MAXG_addKernelPackage(wrap(*kernelDecls), path);
    if (err.isError())
      throw nb::value_error(err.getError());
  }
  // If no user paths were registered, still load built-ins by importing
  // with an empty mojo-pkg-paths list. Detect this case as "module is still
  // empty after the user-paths loop".
  if (libraryPathsSet.empty()) {
    auto err = MAXG_addKernelPackage(wrap(*kernelDecls), {});
    if (err.isError())
      throw nb::value_error(err.getError());
  }
  return *kernelDecls;
}

namespace {
llvm::DenseMap<mlir::TypeID, const std::type_info *> &typeIDRegistry() {
  static llvm::DenseMap<mlir::TypeID, const std::type_info *> registry;
  return registry;
}
} // namespace

void M::Graph::Python::registerTypeID(mlir::TypeID id,
                                      const std::type_info *info) {
  typeIDRegistry()[id] = info;
}

const std::type_info *M::Graph::Python::lookupTypeID(mlir::TypeID id) {
  auto found = typeIDRegistry().find(id);
  if (found == typeIDRegistry().end())
    return nullptr;
  return found->second;
}

using namespace M;

MojoRegistry::MojoRegistry(mlir::ModuleOp module) : deviceInfoMapping(module) {
  for (auto decl : module.getOps<KernelDeclOp>()) {
    kernelByName[decl.getRegistration()].push_back(decl);
    kernelBySymName[decl.getSymName()] = decl;
  }
  for (auto decl : module.getOps<PrimitiveDeclOp>()) {
    llvm::StringRef reg = decl.getRegistration();
    if (!primitiveByName.contains(reg))
      primitiveByName[reg] = decl;
  }
  for (auto decl : module.getOps<ShapeFunctionDeclOp>()) {
    shapeFuncByName[decl.getRegistration()] = decl;
    shapeFuncBySymName[decl.getSymName()] = decl;
  }
}

llvm::ArrayRef<KernelDeclOp>
MojoRegistry::lookupKernel(llvm::StringRef name) const {
  auto it = kernelByName.find(name);
  if (it == kernelByName.end())
    return {};
  return it->second;
}

bool MojoRegistry::containsKernel(llvm::StringRef name) const {
  return kernelByName.contains(name);
}

KernelDeclOp
MojoRegistry::lookupKernelBySymName(llvm::StringRef symName) const {
  KernelDeclOp decl = lookupKernelBySymNameOrNull(symName);
  ASSERT_STREAM(decl, "no kernel registered for '" << symName << "'");
  return decl;
}

KernelDeclOp
MojoRegistry::lookupKernelBySymNameOrNull(llvm::StringRef symName) const {
  return kernelBySymName.lookup(symName);
}

PrimitiveDeclOp MojoRegistry::lookupPrimitive(llvm::StringRef name) const {
  auto it = primitiveByName.find(name);
  ASSERT_STREAM(it != primitiveByName.end(),
                "no primitive registered for '" << name << "'");
  return it->second;
}

bool MojoRegistry::containsPrimitive(llvm::StringRef name) const {
  return primitiveByName.contains(name);
}

ShapeFunctionDeclOp
MojoRegistry::lookupShapeFunction(llvm::StringRef name) const {
  auto it = shapeFuncByName.find(name);
  if (it == shapeFuncByName.end())
    return {};
  return it->second;
}

ShapeFunctionDeclOp
MojoRegistry::lookupShapeFunctionBySymName(llvm::StringRef symName) const {
  ShapeFunctionDeclOp decl = lookupShapeFunctionBySymNameOrNull(symName);
  ASSERT_STREAM(decl, "no shape function registered for sym_name '" << symName
                                                                    << "'");
  return decl;
}

ShapeFunctionDeclOp MojoRegistry::lookupShapeFunctionBySymNameOrNull(
    llvm::StringRef symName) const {
  return shapeFuncBySymName.lookup(symName);
}

bool MojoRegistry::containsShapeFunction(llvm::StringRef name) const {
  return shapeFuncByName.contains(name);
}

static bool deviceInfoMatches(DeviceInfoAttr kernelInfo,
                              DeviceInfoAttr deviceInfo) {
  auto matches = [](llvm::StringRef kernelField, llvm::StringRef runtimeField) {
    return kernelField.empty() || kernelField == runtimeField;
  };
  return matches(kernelInfo.getLabel(), deviceInfo.getLabel()) &&
         matches(kernelInfo.getApi(), deviceInfo.getApi()) &&
         matches(kernelInfo.getArch(), deviceInfo.getArch()) &&
         matches(kernelInfo.getModel(), deviceInfo.getModel());
}

static int deviceInfoSpecificity(DeviceInfoAttr infos) {
  return !infos.getLabel().empty() + !infos.getApi().empty() +
         !infos.getArch().empty() + !infos.getModel().empty();
}

// Whether the kernel's `execute` (or `execute_device`, for a declarative
// kernel) declares a tile-based fused output (_FusedComputeOutputTileTensor or
// _FusedOutputTileTensor), which only lowers on a tile_based_fusion device.
// Transitional, remove per GEX-3941.
static bool kernelDeclaresTileOutput(KernelDeclOp decl) {
  KernelDeclAdaptor adaptor(decl);
  if (!adaptor.executeFunction.has_value())
    return false;
  for (const auto &arg : adaptor.executeFunction->outputArguments) {
    const auto *tensorArg =
        std::get_if<KernelDeclTensorAdaptor>(&arg.underlyingType);
    if (tensorArg &&
        (tensorArg->ioSpec == IOSpec::FusedComputeOutputTileTensor ||
         tensorArg->ioSpec == IOSpec::FusedOutputTileTensor))
      return true;
  }
  return false;
}

// The order candidates win in, compared lexicographically: a user-defined
// kernel beats a built-in one, then more matching device fields beat fewer.
static std::tuple<bool, int> resolutionRank(KernelDeclOp decl) {
  return {decl.getIsUserDefined(),
          deviceInfoSpecificity(decl.getRegistrationDeviceInfo())};
}

ErrorOr<KernelDeclOp>
MojoRegistry::getResolvedKernel(llvm::StringRef name,
                                DeviceRefAttr device) const {
  DeviceInfoAttr deviceInfo = deviceInfoMapping.get(device);
  auto it = kernelByName.find(name);
  ASSERT_STREAM(it != kernelByName.end(),
                "no kernel registered for '" << name << "'");

  llvm::SmallVector<KernelDeclOp> candidates;
  for (KernelDeclOp decl : it->second) {
    if (!deviceInfoMatches(decl.getRegistrationDeviceInfo(), deviceInfo))
      continue;
    // TODO(GEX-3941): on non-tile devices, skip tile-output candidates so the
    // generic (SIMD) registration is selected.
    if (!deviceInfo.getTileBasedFusion() && kernelDeclaresTileOutput(decl))
      continue;
    candidates.push_back(decl);
  }

  if (candidates.empty()) {
    std::string msg;
    llvm::raw_string_ostream os(msg);
    os << "no kernel matching device info for '" << name << "' on device "
       << mlir::Attribute(deviceInfo) << "; candidates:";
    for (KernelDeclOp decl : it->second)
      os << "\n  - " << mlir::Attribute(decl.getRegistrationDeviceInfo())
         << " (" << decl.getSymName() << ")";
    return Error(os.str());
  }

  KernelDeclOp best =
      *llvm::max_element(candidates, [](KernelDeclOp a, KernelDeclOp b) {
        return resolutionRank(a) < resolutionRank(b);
      });
  ASSERT_STREAM(llvm::count_if(candidates,
                               [&](KernelDeclOp decl) {
                                 return resolutionRank(decl) ==
                                        resolutionRank(best);
                               }) == 1,
                "ambiguous kernel resolution for '"
                    << name << "': multiple "
                    << (best.getIsUserDefined() ? "user-defined" : "built-in")
                    << " registrations match the runtime device with equal "
                       "specificity");
  return best;
}

DeviceRefAttr MojoRegistry::getDeviceRefForOp(mlir::Operation *op) {
  if (auto hasDevice = mlir::dyn_cast<MO::MOHasDeviceInterface>(op)) {
    auto devices = hasDevice.getExecutionDevices();
    if (!devices.empty())
      return devices[0];
  }
  return getHostDevice(op->getContext());
}

void M::MOGG::copyKernelDecls(mlir::ModuleOp src, mlir::ModuleOp dst) {
  // Idempotent: a mapping attribute means `dst` is already seeded (e.g. by a
  // prior compile, or in re-imported textual MLIR), and re-cloning the decls
  // would redefine their symbols.
  if (dst->hasAttr(kOpaqueTypeMappingAttrName))
    return;

  mlir::OpBuilder builder(dst.getContext());
  builder.setInsertionPointToEnd(dst.getBody());
  for (auto decl : src.getOps<KernelDeclOp>())
    builder.clone(*decl.getOperation());
  for (auto decl : src.getOps<PrimitiveDeclOp>())
    builder.clone(*decl.getOperation());
  for (auto decl : src.getOps<ShapeFunctionDeclOp>())
    builder.clone(*decl.getOperation());

  // Also carry over the opaque-type mapping so `mogg-rewrite-opaque-types`
  // can run on `dst` without re-loading any packages.
  if (auto mapping = src->getAttrOfType<mlir::DictionaryAttr>(
          kOpaqueTypeMappingAttrName)) {
    dst->setAttr(kOpaqueTypeMappingAttrName, mapping);
  }
}

namespace {

/// Owns the parser state needed to resolve module names and walk the module
/// import directories. One instance serves any number of resolutions, so
/// callers pay the `SharedState` construction cost (config read, dialect
/// preloading) once.
class LITModuleResolver {
public:
  explicit LITModuleResolver(mlir::MLIRContext *ctx)
      : config(ctx, options), sharedState(sourceMgr, config) {}

  /// Resolve `moduleName` to the path of its precompiled binary package.
  ErrorOr<std::string> resolveBinaryPackagePath(llvm::StringRef moduleName) {
    auto modulePath = sharedState.getModuleLoader().resolveModulePath(
        moduleName, llvm::SMLoc());
    if (!modulePath)
      return Error(
          llvm::formatv("Failed to resolve module path for `{}`", moduleName)
              .str());
    if (!modulePath->isPrecompiled()) {
      return Error(
          llvm::formatv("Resolved module path for `{}` to source directory "
                        "`{}` but a .mojoc precompiled file was expected "
                        "instead",
                        moduleName, modulePath->path)
              .str());
    }
    return modulePath->path;
  }

  KGEN::LIT::SharedState &getSharedState() { return sharedState; }

private:
  // Order matters: `config` refers to `options`, `sharedState` to both.
  llvm::SourceMgr sourceMgr;
  KGEN::CompilationOptions options;
  KGEN::LIT::ParserConfig config;
  KGEN::LIT::SharedState sharedState;
};

ErrorOr<llvm::SmallVector<std::string>>
resolveBuiltInKernelPackagePaths(LITModuleResolver &resolver) {
  llvm::SmallVector<std::string> paths;
  for (llvm::StringRef moduleName : kMoggRequiredKernelModuleNames) {
    auto pathOr = resolver.resolveBinaryPackagePath(moduleName);
    if (pathOr.isError())
      return pathOr.takeError();
    paths.push_back(*pathOr);
  }

  // Skipped rather than fatal: a tree without these still has a complete
  // registry of the open-source kernels, and a graph that uses one of their
  // ops fails later with that op unresolved rather than here with every op
  // unresolved.
  for (llvm::StringRef moduleName : kMoggOptionalKernelModuleNames) {
    auto pathOr = resolver.resolveBinaryPackagePath(moduleName);
    if (!pathOr.isError())
      paths.push_back(*pathOr);
  }

  return paths;
}

} // namespace

ErrorOr<llvm::SmallVector<std::string>>
M::MOGG::getBuiltInKernelPackagePaths(mlir::MLIRContext *ctx) {
  LITModuleResolver resolver(ctx);
  return resolveBuiltInKernelPackagePaths(resolver);
}

ErrorOr<llvm::SmallVector<std::string>>
M::MOGG::getKernelPackagePathsForCacheKey(mlir::MLIRContext *ctx) {
  LITModuleResolver resolver(ctx);

  // The built-in packages are required; fail loudly if they don't resolve.
  auto pathsOr = resolveBuiltInKernelPackagePaths(resolver);
  if (pathsOr.isError())
    return pathsOr.takeError();
  llvm::SmallVector<std::string> paths = std::move(*pathsOr);

  // Collect every binary package visible on the import path; the header
  // comment explains why their contents must reach the cache key.
  llvm::StringSet<> seen;
  resolver.getSharedState().getModuleLoader().traverseImportDirectories(
      /*importBufferFileId=*/0, [&](llvm::StringRef dir) {
        std::error_code ec;
        for (llvm::sys::fs::directory_iterator it(dir, ec), e; it != e && !ec;
             it.increment(ec)) {
          const std::string &path = it->path();
          if (M::Filesystem::isMojoBinaryPackagePath(path))
            seen.insert(path);
        }
        return mlir::WalkResult::advance();
      });
  // Append the collected packages, minus the built-ins already in `paths`,
  // sorted for a deterministic order independent of directory iteration.
  llvm::SmallVector<std::string> found;
  for (const auto &entry : seen)
    if (!llvm::is_contained(paths, entry.getKey()))
      found.push_back(entry.getKey().str());
  llvm::sort(found);
  llvm::append_range(paths, found);
  return paths;
}

namespace M::MOGG {

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Custom op parameter validation using KernelDeclOp
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

static bool isStaticStringLoweredPattern(Type ty) {
  auto structTy = dyn_cast<KGEN::StructType>(ty);
  if (!structTy || structTy.getIsParamPack())
    return false;

  std::optional<SmallVector<Type>> elementTypes = structTy.getElementTypes();
  if (!elementTypes || elementTypes->size() != 2)
    return false;

  auto ptrTy = dyn_cast<KGEN::PointerType>((*elementTypes)[0]);
  if (!ptrTy || !isa<KGEN::NoneType>(ptrTy.getElementType()))
    return false;

  return isa<IndexType>((*elementTypes)[1]);
}

static bool isLITStaticStringType(Type ty) {
  auto structTy = KGEN::sugarDynCast<KGEN::LIT::StructType>(ty);
  if (!structTy) {
    if (auto ref = KGEN::sugarDynCast<KGEN::LIT::RefType>(ty)) {
      structTy =
          KGEN::sugarDynCast<KGEN::LIT::StructType>(ref.getElementType());
    }
  }
  if (!structTy)
    return false;

  auto symbol = structTy.getSymbol();
  std::string symbolName =
      llvm::formatv("{}::{}", symbol.getRootReference().strref(),
                    symbol.getLeafReference().strref())
          .str();
  if (symbolName != kMojoInternalStaticStringTypeName)
    return false;

  auto paramValues = structTy.getParamValues();
  if (paramValues.empty())
    return false;

  TypedAttr originAttr = paramValues[0];
  if (KGEN::sugarIsa<KGEN::ParamDeclRefAttr>(originAttr))
    return false;

  return true;
}

static StringAttr maybeDemangleName(StringAttr name) {
  StringRef demangled = KGEN::demangleParameterName(name);
  if (demangled != name)
    return StringAttr::get(name.getContext(), demangled);
  return name;
}

/// Return the "root::leaf" name of a parameter's declared struct type, or
/// null for non-structs. Distinguishes e.g. `std::StaticString` from
/// `std::StringSpan`, which the bare KGEN type does not.
static StringAttr getParamTypeName(MLIRContext *ctx, Type paramType) {
  if (auto ref = KGEN::sugarDynCast<KGEN::LIT::RefType>(paramType))
    paramType = ref.getElementType();
  auto structTy = KGEN::sugarDynCast<KGEN::LIT::StructType>(paramType);
  if (!structTy)
    return nullptr;
  auto sym = structTy.getSymbol();
  return StringAttr::get(ctx, llvm::formatv("{}::{}",
                                            sym.getRootReference().strref(),
                                            sym.getLeafReference().strref())
                                  .str());
}

static ErrorOrSuccess validateCustomOpParameters(const MojoRegistry &registry,
                                                 MO::CustomOp customOp) {
  if (!registry.containsKernel(customOp.getSymbol())) {
    return Error(
        llvm::formatv("no kernel registered for '{}'", customOp.getSymbol())
            .str());
  }
  auto declOpOr =
      registry.getResolvedKernel(customOp.getSymbol(), customOp.getDevice());
  if (declOpOr.isError())
    return declOpOr.takeError();
  KernelDeclOp declOp = *declOpOr;
  // The user-supplied `parameters` dict on `mo.custom` can target struct-level
  // compile-time params (e.g. `[interleaved: Bool]`) or any fn-level params
  // declared on the registered functions. Collect struct params first to
  // mirror Mojo's merged `@register` parameter layout, then union the param
  // lists from each function variant (some kernels declare params only on
  // `elementwise` instead of `execute`).
  SmallVector<KGEN::ParamDeclAttr> inputParamsVec;
  llvm::SmallDenseSet<StringRef> seen;
  auto append = [&](KGEN::ParamDeclAttr p) {
    if (seen.insert(p.getName().strref()).second)
      inputParamsVec.push_back(p);
  };
  for (auto a : declOp.getStructParams())
    append(cast<KGEN::ParamDeclAttr>(a));
  auto appendFn = [&](std::optional<MOGG::KernelFunctionAttr> fn) {
    if (!fn)
      return;
    for (auto a : fn->getParams())
      append(cast<KGEN::ParamDeclAttr>(a));
  };
  appendFn(declOp.getExecute());
  appendFn(declOp.getElementwise());
  if (registry.containsShapeFunction(declOp.getRegistration()))
    appendFn(
        registry.lookupShapeFunction(declOp.getRegistration()).getFunction());
  ArrayRef<KGEN::ParamDeclAttr> inputParams = inputParamsVec;

  struct MojoParameterAdaptor {
    KGEN::ParamDeclAttr attr;
    StringAttr typeName;
  };
  llvm::DenseMap<StringAttr, MojoParameterAdaptor> paramDecls;

  MLIRContext *ctx = customOp.getContext();
  for (auto param : inputParams)
    paramDecls[maybeDemangleName(param.getName())] = {
        param, getParamTypeName(ctx, param.getType())};

  for (const NamedAttribute &namedAttr : customOp.getParameters()) {
    auto it = paramDecls.find(namedAttr.getName());
    if (it == paramDecls.end()) {
      return Error(llvm::formatv(
          "parameter '{}' supplied to custom op '{}' does not exist in the "
          "corresponding Mojo code",
          namedAttr.getName().strref(), customOp.getSymbol()));
    }

    auto [customParamDecl, typeName] = it->second;
    Attribute mlirParamAttr = namedAttr.getValue();

    Type kgenParamTy = customParamDecl.getType();
    auto asIntegerType = dyn_cast<IntegerType>(kgenParamTy);

    if (isa<IndexType>(kgenParamTy)) {
      auto asInt = dyn_cast<IntegerAttr>(mlirParamAttr);
      if (asInt == nullptr) {
        return Error(llvm::formatv(
            "failed to convert custom op '{}' attribute '{}' with value '{}' "
            "to the proper Mojo parameter type: expected an integer type",
            customOp.getSymbol(), customParamDecl.getName(), mlirParamAttr));
      }
    } else if (asIntegerType && asIntegerType.getWidth() == 1) {
      auto asBool = dyn_cast<BoolAttr>(mlirParamAttr);
      if (asBool == nullptr) {
        return Error(llvm::formatv(
            "failed to convert custom op '{}' attribute '{}' with value '{}' "
            "to the proper Mojo parameter type: expected an boolean type",
            customOp.getSymbol(), customParamDecl.getName(), mlirParamAttr));
      }
    } else if (isa<KGEN::StringType>(kgenParamTy)) {
      if (typeName && typeName == MOGG::kMojoInternalStaticStringTypeName) {
        [[maybe_unused]] auto asString = dyn_cast<StringAttr>(mlirParamAttr);
        ASSERT_STREAM(asString != nullptr,
                      "failed to convert custom op attribute "
                          << mlirParamAttr
                          << " to expected mojo kernel param type "
                          << kgenParamTy << ": expected string type");
      } else if (typeName) {
        return Error(llvm::formatv(
            "in custom op '{}' attribute '{}' uses a string-like type that is "
            "not supported. Use type StaticString instead to define a string "
            "parameter for a custom op.",
            customOp.getSymbol(), customParamDecl.getName()));
      } else {
        [[maybe_unused]] auto asString = dyn_cast<StringAttr>(mlirParamAttr);
        ASSERT_STREAM(asString != nullptr,
                      "failed to convert custom op attribute "
                          << mlirParamAttr
                          << " to expected mojo kernel param type "
                          << kgenParamTy << ": expected string type");
      }
    } else if (isa<KGEN::DTypeType>(kgenParamTy)) {
      auto asType = dyn_cast<TypeAttr>(mlirParamAttr);
      if (asType == nullptr) {
        return Error(llvm::formatv(
            "failed to convert custom op '{}' attribute '{}' with value '{}' "
            "to the proper Mojo parameter type: expected a DType",
            customOp.getSymbol(), customParamDecl.getName(), mlirParamAttr));
      }
    } else if (typeName && typeName == MOGG::kMojoDtypeTypeName) {
      auto asType = dyn_cast<TypeAttr>(mlirParamAttr);
      if (asType == nullptr) {
        return Error(llvm::formatv(
            "failed to convert custom op '{}' attribute '{}' with value '{}' "
            "to the proper Mojo parameter type: expected a DType",
            customOp.getSymbol(), customParamDecl.getName(), mlirParamAttr));
      }
    } else if (typeName && typeName == MOGG::kMojoStaticStringTypeName) {
      auto asString = dyn_cast<StringAttr>(mlirParamAttr);
      if (asString == nullptr) {
        return Error(llvm::formatv(
            "failed to convert custom op '{}' attribute '{}' with value '{}' "
            "to the proper Mojo parameter type: expected string type",
            customOp.getSymbol(), customParamDecl.getName(), mlirParamAttr));
      }
    } else if (typeName &&
               typeName == MOGG::kMojoInternalStaticStringTypeName) {
      bool isStaticString = isLITStaticStringType(kgenParamTy) ||
                            isStaticStringLoweredPattern(kgenParamTy);
      if (isStaticString) {
        auto asString = dyn_cast<StringAttr>(mlirParamAttr);
        if (asString == nullptr) {
          return Error(llvm::formatv(
              "failed to convert custom op '{}' attribute '{}' with value "
              "'{}' to the proper Mojo parameter type: expected string type",
              customOp.getSymbol(), customParamDecl.getName(), mlirParamAttr));
        }
      } else {
        return Error(llvm::formatv(
            "in custom op '{}' attribute '{}' uses type StringSpan. Use type "
            "StaticString instead to define a string parameter for a custom "
            "op.",
            customOp.getSymbol(), customParamDecl.getName()));
      }
    } else if (typeName && (typeName == MOGG::kMojoInternalDpsSimdTypeName)) {
      // `Float32` (SIMD[f32, 1]) params arrive as the same rank-0 f32
      // elements attribute the RMS norm ops use for their scalar
      // attributes (see `MO_F32ScalarAttr`); integer SIMD params arrive
      // as `IntegerAttr`. Only f32 matches here: `MO_F32ScalarAttr` and
      // the Python side (`rms_norm._float32_attr`) are f32-only. A float
      // value against an integer SIMD param is not caught here — this
      // branch keys on the SIMD kind, not the element type — and fails
      // later, at Mojo elaboration.
      if (auto elems = dyn_cast<ElementsAttr>(mlirParamAttr);
          elems && elems.getShapedType().getRank() == 0 &&
          elems.getElementType().isF32())
        continue;
      auto asInt = dyn_cast<IntegerAttr>(mlirParamAttr);
      if (asInt == nullptr) {
        return Error(llvm::formatv(
            "failed to convert custom op '{}' attribute '{}' with value '{}' "
            "to the proper Mojo parameter type: expected an integer type",
            customOp.getSymbol(), customParamDecl.getName(), mlirParamAttr));
      }
    } else if (typeName && typeName == MOGG::kMojoInternalDpsBoolTypeName) {
      auto asBool = dyn_cast<BoolAttr>(mlirParamAttr);
      if (asBool == nullptr) {
        return Error(llvm::formatv(
            "failed to convert custom op '{}' attribute '{}' with value '{}' "
            "to the proper Mojo parameter type: expected a boolean type",
            customOp.getSymbol(), customParamDecl.getName(), mlirParamAttr));
      }
    } else if (typeName &&
               (typeName == MOGG::kMojoInternalStringSliceTypeName ||
                typeName == MOGG::kMojoStringTypeName)) {
      std::string userTypeName =
          typeName == MOGG::kMojoInternalStringSliceTypeName ? "StringSpan"
                                                             : "String";
      return Error(llvm::formatv(
          "in custom op '{}' attribute '{}' uses type {}. Use type "
          "StaticString instead to define a string parameter for a custom op.",
          customOp.getSymbol(), customParamDecl.getName(), userTypeName));
    } else if (!typeName && isStaticStringLoweredPattern(kgenParamTy)) {
      auto asString = dyn_cast<StringAttr>(mlirParamAttr);
      if (asString == nullptr) {
        return Error(llvm::formatv(
            "failed to convert custom op '{}' attribute '{}' with value '{}' "
            "to the proper Mojo parameter type: expected string type",
            customOp.getSymbol(), customParamDecl.getName(), mlirParamAttr));
      }
    } else {
      return Error(llvm::formatv(
          "failed to convert custom op '{}' attribute '{}' with value '{}' to "
          "the proper Mojo parameter type: no type conversion found to type "
          "'{}'",
          customOp.getSymbol(), customParamDecl.getName(), mlirParamAttr,
          kgenParamTy));
    }
  }

  return success();
}

} // namespace M::MOGG

ErrorOrSuccess M::MOGG::verifyCustomOp(const MojoRegistry &registry,
                                       mlir::Operation *op) {
  MojoOperationInfo info = getMojoOperationInfo(op);
  if (info.operationName.empty())
    return Error(llvm::formatv("operation '{}' has no registered Mojo kernel",
                               op->getName().getStringRef())
                     .str());

  if (!registry.containsKernel(info.operationName)) {
    return Error(
        llvm::formatv("no kernel registered for '{}'", info.operationName)
            .str());
  }

  // Catch MO-vs-kernel operand type mismatches before lowering emits Mojo.
  DeviceRefAttr device = MojoRegistry::getDeviceRefForOp(op);
  auto declOpOr = registry.getResolvedKernel(info.operationName, device);
  if (declOpOr.isError())
    return declOpOr.takeError();
  KernelDeclAdaptor adaptor(*declOpOr);
  if (adaptor.isReduction()) {
    StringRef name = info.operationName;
    if (op->getNumOperands() != 1)
      return Error(llvm::formatv("reduction '{}' takes one input, got {}", name,
                                 op->getNumOperands())
                       .str());
    size_t numFields = adaptor.reduction->getOutputs().size();
    if (op->getNumResults() != numFields)
      return Error(llvm::formatv("reduction '{}' has {} output fields, but the "
                                 "op has {} results",
                                 name, numFields, op->getNumResults())
                       .str());
    ErrorOr<int64_t> axis =
        MO::getReductionAxis(op->getOperand(0), op->getResultTypes());
    if (axis.isError())
      return Error(
          llvm::formatv("reduction '{}' {}", name, axis.getError()).str());
  } else {
    if (!adaptor.executeFunction)
      return Error(llvm::formatv("kernel '{}' has no execute function",
                                 info.operationName)
                       .str());
    auto mappingOr =
        mapMOOpToKernelImplementation(op, *adaptor.executeFunction);
    if (mappingOr.isError())
      return mappingOr.takeError();
  }

  // For mo.custom, additionally check that user-supplied params match the
  // kernel's compile-time parameter list (struct + fn-level).
  if (auto custom = mlir::dyn_cast<MO::CustomOp>(op))
    return validateCustomOpParameters(registry, custom);

  return success();
}

ErrorOrSuccess M::MOGG::verifyCustomOp(mlir::ModuleOp kernelDecls,
                                       mlir::Operation *op) {
  MojoRegistry registry(kernelDecls);
  return verifyCustomOp(registry, op);
}
